### `app/[locale]/dashboard/all-users/page.tsx:213` — **INFO A13: font-bold on non-hero/meta text**

```
<span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-pill bg-s-coral/10 text-s-coral text-[10px] font-bold">
```

_Per LOCKFILE §2.5 rule A13 (card text hierarchy, V3-D346): font-bold (700) is reserved for Hero H1. Card/list-item names use font-medium (500) as the single ink anchor; meta uses font-normal text-s-ink-2. Drop font-bold -> font-medium (if this is the name) or font-normal text-s-ink-2 (if this is meta). Or use the <CardName>/<CardMeta> primitives._

### `app/[locale]/dashboard/settings/page.tsx:527` — **INFO A13: font-bold on non-hero/meta text**

```
<p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-s-ink/30 uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">Vorschau für Kunden</p>
```

_Per LOCKFILE §2.5 rule A13 (card text hierarchy, V3-D346): font-bold (700) is reserved for Hero H1. Card/list-item names use font-medium (500) as the single ink anchor; meta uses font-normal text-s-ink-2. Drop font-bold -> font-medium (if this is the name) or font-normal text-s-ink-2 (if this is meta). Or use the <CardName>/<CardMeta> primitives._

### `app/[locale]/dev/primitives/page.tsx:1090` — **INFO A8: non-canonical tracking**

```
<div className="font-body font-bold text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-s-ink-3 mb-3.5">
```

_`tracking-[0.16em]` not in canonical set ['-.005', '-.01', '-.015', '-.02', '-0.005', '-0.01', '-0.015', '-0.02', '.06', '.08', '0', '0.06', '0.08']. Per LOCKFILE §2.5, valid values are -0.02 / -0.015 / -0.01 / -0.005 / 0 / 0.06 / 0.08. Pick nearest canonical._

### `app/[locale]/dev/primitives/page.tsx:1090` — **INFO A13: font-bold on non-hero/meta text**

```
<div className="font-body font-bold text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-s-ink-3 mb-3.5">
```

_Per LOCKFILE §2.5 rule A13 (card text hierarchy, V3-D346): font-bold (700) is reserved for Hero H1. Card/list-item names use font-medium (500) as the single ink anchor; meta uses font-normal text-s-ink-2. Drop font-bold -> font-medium (if this is the name) or font-normal text-s-ink-2 (if this is meta). Or use the <CardName>/<CardMeta> primitives._
